2020年受各方面因素影響 ，全世界經濟出現動     Affected by various factors in 2020, the world economy has been in
盪 ，對整個市場的供需造成較大影響 ，氟硅行業     turmoil, which had a great impact on the supply and demand of the
市場也不可避免的受到衝擊 。2020年內 ，氟硅行   entire market, and the fluorosilicone market was inevitably impacted.
業大量產品陷入價格下滑趨勢 ，行業利潤受到壓      In 2020, the price of a large number of products in the fluorosilicone
縮 ，經營形勢堪憂 。然而 ，本集團通過良好的管    industry was falling, and the profit margins of the industry were
理 ，發揮產業鏈優勢及園區配套一體化優勢保證      suppressed, resulting in a difficult operating environment. However,
了正常的生產運行 ，並全力搶佔市場 ，在艱難的     through adequate management, the Group has leveraged its
時期創造了良好的業績 ，繼續鞏固了本集團行業      advantages in industry chain as well as comprehensive support of its
龍頭的地位 。                     industry park, and has ensured normal production and operation. The
                            Group has also made every endeavour to expand the market share,
                            which allowed us to achieve an outstanding performance in difficult
                            times and continue to strengthen the leading position of the Group in
                            the industry.

 2020年初 ，國內「新冠肺炎」疫情爆發 ，嚴重        At the beginning of 2020, the outbreak of “COVID-19” in China
 影響了全國人民的正常生活 。受疫情影響 ，           severely affected the normal life of the people nationwide.

 響 。在此嚴峻的環境下 ，本集團管理層迅            to the pandemic, and logistics and transportation were greatly
 速意識到疫情防控的重要性 ，本集團各子公            affected. Under such severe circumstances, the management
 司 、各部門迅速採取行動 ，制定防控疫情方           of the Group promptly realized the importance of pandemic
 案 ，全面嚴格執行 ，保障了集團正常的生            prevention and control. The subsidiaries and departments of the
 產運行；本集團還積極協調國內疫情防控部             Group took prompt action to formulate a plan for the pandemic
 門 ，盡可能解決原材料和產品運輸問題 ，            prevention and control, and fully and strictly implemented it to
 降低物流運輸對本集團供應鏈及銷售網的影             ensure the normal production and operation of the Group. The
 響；針對不景氣的需求市場 ，本集團提前預            Group also actively coordinated with the domestic pandemic
 判 ，進一步加大銷售力度 ，拓展客戶 ，搶           prevention and control departments to resolve the transportation
 佔市場 ，開拓高端產品銷售 ，使集團在市            of raw materials and products to the utmost, so as to reduce the

 （下稱「未來氫能」）於上海證券交易所科創板              Hydrogen Energy”) would be independently issued and listed on
 獨立發行上市 。未來氫能公司生產的燃料電               the Science and Technology Innovation Board of Shanghai Stock
 池膜是氫能源設備的核心構成部分 ，其技術               Exchange. The fuel cell membrane produced by Future Hydrogen
 世界領先 ，在氫能源將成為未來儲能 、能源              Energy is the core component of hydrogen energy equipment.
 供給發展趨勢的行業形勢下 ，未來氫能公司               Future Hydrogen Energy has world-leading technology. As
 的分拆上市如果得以落實 ，預期將可加快推               hydrogen energy will become the development trend of energy
 進未來氫能的業務發展 ，拓寬其融資渠道 ，              storage and energy supply in the future, the spinoff and listing of
 提升其發展潛力 、競爭力和行業地位 。                Future Hydrogen Energy, if implemented, is expected to accelerate
                                    the development of future hydrogen energy business, broaden
                                    its financing channels, and also to enhance its development

匯率波動風險及相關對沖活動                        Exposure to Fluctuations in Exchange Rates and Related
                                     Hedges
本集團的功能貨幣為人民幣 ，且大多數交易以人               The Group’s functional currency is RMB with most of the transactions
民幣結算 。然而 ，本集團自海外客戶賺取收益及              settled in RMB. However, foreign currencies (mainly United States dollars)
向海外供應商購買機器及設備結算時均收取╱支                were received/paid when the Group earned revenue from overseas
付外幣（主要為美元）。                          customers and when settling purchases of machinery and equipment
                                     from the overseas suppliers.


為減少持有外幣的風險 ，本集團經考慮不久將來               In order to reduce the risk of holding foreign currencies, the Group normally
的外幣付款安排後 ，通常會在款項收訖後隨即將               converts the foreign currencies into RMB upon receipt while taking into
外幣兌換成人民幣 。                           account its foreign currencies payment schedule in the near future.

 此為目標公司的主要股東 。彼岸時代的66%股權        therefore is a substantial shareholder of the Target Company. Paramita
 由一位執行董事的聯繫人持有 ，因此 ，根據《上        is held as to 66% by an associate of an executive Director. Therefore, the
 市規則》第14A.28(2)條 ，注資構成本公司的關連交   capital injection constitutes a connected transaction of the Company
 易。                             under Rule 14A.28(2) of the Listing Rules.

該交易的進一步詳情載於日期為2020年5月28日的       Further details of the transaction are set out in the announcement
公告 。                            dated 28 May 2020.

東岳集團有限公司（「本公司」）董事會（「董事會」）    The board of Directors (the “Board”) of Dongyue Group Limited (the
肩負為本公司股東（「股東」）創造價值及帶來最大      “Company”) recognizes their mission of creating values and maximizing
回報的使命 ，同時履行其企業責任 。為此 ，本公     returns to the shareholders of the Company (the “Shareholders”), while
司矢志提升及保持最高水準的企業管治 。          at the same time fulfilling their corporate responsibilities. To this end,
                             we strive to promote and uphold the highest standard of corporate
                             governance.


董事會已檢討本公司的企業管治常規 ，並採納及       The Board has reviewed the corporate governance practices of
改進各項程序及文書處理方式 ，詳情載於本報        the Company with the adoption and improvement of the various
告 。據董事所盡知及盡信 ，本公司於截至2020年    procedures and documentation which are detailed in this report. To
12月31日止年度及截至本報告日期採納並一直遵      the best knowledge and belief of the Directors, the Company has
守上市規則附錄14所載的企業管治守則（「守則」）     adopted and complied with the code provisions of the Corporate
的守則條文 ，惟關於主席與行政總裁的角色偏離       Governance Code contained in Appendix 14 of the Listing Rules (the
守則除外 ，有關詳情披露於本報告下文相關段        “Code”) during the year ended 31 December 2020 and up to the date
落。                           of this report, except for deviation regarding the roles of chairman and
                             chief executive officer, the details of which have been disclosed in the
                             relevant paragraphs below in this report.

和表現 。董事會轉授權力和責任予本集團管理人       businesses, strategic direction and performance. The Board delegates
員 ，以管理本集團 。此外 ，董事會亦已轉授若干     the authority and responsibility to the Group’s management for the

主席與行政總裁的角色                  Roles of chairman and chief executive officer
守則第A.2.1條規定 ，主席與行政總裁的角色應有   Code A.2.1 of the Code stipulates that the roles of chairman and chief
區分 ，且不應由同一人兼任 。張建宏先生目前身     executive officer should be separate and should not be performed
兼本公司主席與行政總裁 。董事會認為 ，由一人     by the same individual. Mr. Zhang Jianhong is both the Chairman
同時兼任主席及行政總裁可令本集團更能貫徹強       and Chief Executive Officer of the Company. The Board believes that
勢領導 ，且在業務策劃上更具效率 。此外 ，董     vesting the roles of both Chairman and Chief Executive Officer in the
事會認為 ，在由多名經驗豐富且具才幹的人士所      same position provides the Group with stronger and more consistent
組成董事會密切監控下 ，此架構不會影響權力平      leadership and allows for more effective planning. Further, the Board
衡 。董事會對張先生充滿信心 ，並深信現時安排     considers that this structure will not impair the balance of power, which
對本集團的業務前景最為有利 。             has been closely monitored by the Board, which comprises experienced
                            and high caliber individuals. The Board has full confidence in Mr. Zhang
                            and believes that the current arrangement is beneficial to the business
                            prospect of the Group.

提名委員會負責 。提名委員會就相關事項（包括      been delegated to the Company’s Nomination Committee, which
本公司董事的委任及重新委任）向董事會提供建       would make recommendations to the Board on relevant matters
議 。董事會認為 ，預期新任董事應具備有關範疇     including the appointment or reappointment of the Directors of the
的專業知識 ，以為本公司作出貢獻 ，且有足夠時     Company. The Board considers that the new Director(s) is expected to
間參與本公司的決策程序 。               have expertise in relevant area to make contribution to the Company

此外 ，有關董事會成員多元化政策的概要載列如            In addition, a summary of the Policy for the diversity of the Board is set
下：                                out as follows:


A.   政策聲明                         A.   Policy Statement
     本公司明白並深信建立一個多元的董事會的               The Company recognizes and embraces the benefits of having a
     裨益良多 。一個真正多元的董事會可容入並              diverse Board. A truly diverse Board will include and make good
     善用董事的不同技能 、地區及行業經驗 、背             use of differences in the skills, regional and industry experience,
     景 、種族 、性別及其他資歷 。此等差異將             background, race, gender and other qualities of Directors. These
     用作決定董事會的最佳組合 。董事會所有的              differences will be taken into account in determining the optimum
     委任均以用人唯才為原則 ，並於考慮人選時              composition of the Board. All Board appointments will be based
     以客觀條件充分顧及董事會成員多元化的裨               on meritocracy, and candidates will be considered against
     益。                                objective criteria, having due regard for the benefits of diversity of
                                       the Board.


B.   可測量目標                        B.   Measurable Objectives
     1. 提名委員會將就實施董事會成員多元化              1.  The Nomination Committee will discuss and agree annually
        每年討論及議定可測量目標 ，並向董                  measurable objectives for implementing diversity on the
        事會作出建議以供採納 。                        Board and recommend to the Board for adoption.


     2.   本公司旨在建立並維持董事會董事在技            2.   The Company aims to build and maintain a Board with
          能 、經驗 、知識 、專長 、文化 、獨立             a diversity of Directors, in terms of skills, experiences,
          身份 、年齡及性別方面的多元化 。                 knowledge, expertise, culture, independence, age and
                                            gender.

董事會聲明                            Board Statement
本聲明旨在闡釋東岳集團董事會對環境 、社會及           The purpose of this statement is to explain the governance and
管治相關工作的治理和監督責任 。                 supervision responsibilities of the Board of the Dongyue Group with
                                 respect to environmental, social and governance related work.


東岳集團有限公司深知良好的公司治理和風險管            Dongyue Group Limited is well aware of the importance of good
理流程的重要性 ，包括對公司可持續發展至關重           corporate governance and risk management processes, including
要的環境 、社會及管治事項管理 。集團致力於           the management of environmental, social and governance issues
提高可持續業務實踐 ，建立健全ESG管治機制 ，         that are critical to the sustainable development of the Company. The
努力與環境和社會和諧相融 ，創造長期穩定的環           Group is committed to enhancing sustainable business practices and
境 、社會以及企業價值 。                    establishing a sound ESG governance mechanism. The Group also
                                 strives to integrate with the environment and society to create long-
                                 term and stable environmental, social and corporate values.

1. 責任運營                             1. Responsible Operation
 東岳集團秉持依法經營 、規範運作 、安全第                 Dongyue Group adhered to the principle of legal and compliance
 一 、穩健發展的經營方針 ，將責任運營融                  operations, safety first and stable development, integrated
 入日常管理和對本集團的未來規劃中 ，始終                  responsible operation into the daily operation and future planning
 遵循誠信合規的運營理念 ，持續提升產品質                  of the Group, and persistently followed the operation concept
 量 ，提供優質產品與服務 ，積極履行對供應                 of integrity and compliance. The Group continued to improve
 商 、客戶等利益相關方的職責 ，協調 、管                 product quality and provide quality products and services, actively
 理各方資源保障供應 。                           performed its responsibilities towards stakeholders such as
                                       suppliers and customers, and coordinated and managed various
                                       resources to assure supply.


 1.1 誠信合規                           1.1 Integrity and Compliance
   東 岳 集 團 以「 厚 德 載 物 ， 誠 信 為 本 」      Dongyue Group adhered to its business ethics of “embracing all
   作為企業的道德理念 ，並在本集團日                   with a high virtue, building itself on integrity” and integrated this
   常運營中融入該理念 。在打造卓越商                   ethics into the daily operation of the Group. In the process of
   譽過程中 ，本集團恪守守法合規的信                   building an excellent goodwill, the Group adhered to the notion
   念，要求所有員工嚴格遵守行業規                     of legal compliance. The Group required all employees to strictly
   範 ，全面提高依法治企的能力 。                    comply with the industry norm, and improved its capability of
                                       corporate governance according to the laws in an all-round way.


   為打造清廉的工作環境 ，本集團嚴格                   To build a corruption-free working environment, the Group strictly
   遵守《關於辦理商業賄賂刑事案件適用                   comply with the Opinions on Issues Concerning the Application
   法律若干問題的意見》      《關於辦理貪污賄            of Law in the Handling of Criminal Cases of Commercial Briberies,
   賂刑事案件適用法律若干問題的解釋》                   Interpretation on Several Issues Concerning the Application of
   等相關法律法規 ，不斷健全反腐敗管                   Law in the Handling of Criminal Cases of Embezzlement and
   治機制 ，持續加強制度建設 。對內 ，                 Bribery and other laws and regulations, and continued to improve
   公司制定《東岳集團風險管理手冊》，                   its governance mechanism against corruption and strengthened
   健全風險管理體系 ，識別和管理公司                   its institutional improvement on an ongoing basis. Internally,
   風險；制定《東岳集團反舞弊行為管理                   the Company developed the Dongyue Group Risk Management
   規 定 》， 規 範 員 工 職 業 行 為 ， 防 止 舞      Manual to improve its risk management system, and to identify
   弊；制定《東岳集團幹部春節八禁令》，                  and control the Company’s risk; developed the Dongyue Group

 4.1 氣候變化風險分析             4.1 Climate Change Risk Analysis
   氣候變化給水資源 、生態環境 、能源          Climate change poses many challenges to water resources,
   發展等領域帶來了諸多挑戰 。面對刻           ecological environment, energy development and other
   不容緩的氣候變化形勢 ，東岳集團從           fields. With the imminent impacts of climate change,
   企業自身出發 ，將對氣候風險的考量           Dongyue Group set out itself to embed the consideration of
   融入現有風險管理體系 ，積極識別並           climate risks into its existing risk management system, and
   評估氣候變化面臨的風險和機遇 。            actively identify and evaluate the risks and opportunities
                               associated with climate change.




                                           Dongyue Group Limited Annual Report 2020       93
環境 、社會及管治報告
ENVIRONMENTAL, SOCIAL AND GOVERNANCE REPORT




       •   面臨的氣候變化風險
           轉型風險  政策風險                 我國積極參與全球氣候治理 ，踐行《京都議定書》 《巴黎協定》的承諾 ，將應對
                                      氣候變化作為新時代現代化建設的一項重要目標和內容 。2020年 ，我國將提
                                      高國家自主減排貢獻力度 ，並承諾於2030年前達到碳峰值 ，2060年前實現碳
                                      中和 。隨著相關政策的收緊以及聯交所等機構對氣候風險識別的披露期望 ，
                                      東岳集團也將受到監管部門與溫室氣體排放有關的限制 ，導致合規成本的增
                                      加。


                                      2020年 ，國家生態環境部發文將於2021年起實施《碳排放權交易管理辦法（試
                                      行）》，啟動全國碳排放權交易系統 ，實現碳中和承諾 。中國開始從國家層面
                                      將溫室氣體控排責任壓實到企業 ，利用市場機制倒逼企業進行技術和工藝創
                                      新 ，減少碳排放強度 。雖然首批納入碳交易試點的為發電企業 ，考慮到未來
                                      控排範圍的持續擴大 ，東岳集團應提前部署低碳發展方案 ，實施技術創新 ，
                                      做好應對政策風險的準備 。

       •   Exposure to climate change risk


           Transition   Policy        China has been actively participating in global climate governance and fulfilling
           risks        risks         the commitments of the Kyoto Protocol and the Paris Agreement, and regards
                                      tackling climate change as an important goal and content of modernization in
                                      the new era. In 2020, China scaled up its nationally determined contributions, and
                                      pledged to peak carbon dioxide emissions by 2030 and achieve carbon neutrality
                                      by 2060. With the tightening of relevant policies and the expectations on the
                                      disclosure of the identification of climate risk by the Stock Exchange and other
                                      institutions, Dongyue Group will also be subject to restrictions on greenhouse gas
                                      emissions by regulatory authorities, leading to increased compliance costs.


                                      In 2020, the Ministry of Ecology and Environment announced the implementation
                                      of the Administrative Measures for Carbon Emission Trading (Trial) from 2021,
                                      pursuant to which a national carbon emission trading system will be launched
                                      with an aim to meet its carbon neutrality commitment. China began to transfer
                                      the responsibility for greenhouse gas control and emission reduction from the
                                      national level to enterprises, forcing enterprises to carry out technological and
                                      process innovations by using the market mechanism to reduce the intensity of
                                      carbon emissions. Although the first batch of carbon trading pilot enterprises are
                                      power enterprises, taking into account the continuous expansion of the scope of
                                      carbon emission control in the future, Dongyue Group shall develop low-carbon
                                      development plans in advance and implement technological innovations to be
                                      prepared for policy risks.

                     市場風險             客戶對綠色產品方案和低碳足跡產品的需求不斷增加 ，尤其是歐洲的客戶對
                                      製冷劑要求的生產標準較高 。如果東岳集團沒有考慮到這一需求 ，在生產氟
                                      材料 、有機硅材料時廢氣排放超標 ，或者未能有效地開發新型可持續環保產
                                      品方案 ，市場銷售額可能會受影響 。
                     Market risks     Customers’ demand for green product solutions and low-carbon footprint
                                      products continues to increase, especially for European customers, who require
                                      higher production standards for refrigerants. If Dongyue Group does not take
                                      this into consideration and has excessive exhaust emissions in the production of
                                      fluorine materials and organic silicone materials or fails to effectively develop new
                                      sustainable environmentally friendly product solutions, its market sales may be
                                      adversely affected.


    實體風險             物理風險             氣候變化引發的暴雨 、颱風 、沙塵暴等極端天氣或水資源短缺等長期氣候變
                                      化 ，會潛在得影響上游供應鏈及原材料的運輸 ，破壞和損毀生產設備 ，增加
                                      公司運營成本 ，並導致產能下降或停運 ，影響勞動力管理和規劃 。
    Physical risks   Physical risks   Extreme weather such as heavy rain, typhoons and sandstorms, or long-term
                                      climate changes such as water shortages caused by climate change will potentially
                                      affect the upstream supply chain and the transportation of raw materials, and
                                      damage and destroy the production facilities. This may lead to an increase in
                                      the Company’s operating costs and a decline in production capacity or even
                                      production suspension, affecting labor management and planning.

廢物管理制度》《危險廢物責任制度》等                             System, Hazardous Waste Management System, Hazardous

                                               methods to avoid environmental pollution incidents.


東岳集團制定《危險廢物規範化管理指                              Dongyue Group formulated the Standardized Management
標體系》，通過對管理台賬的完善 ，加                             Index System for Hazardous Wastes. Through the
強各分子公司的固廢處理監督考核及                               improvement of management account, the Company
危險廢物管理力度 。公司在2020年疫                            strengthened the supervision and assessment of solid waste
情期間完善內部管理考核計劃 ，按照                              treatment as well as hazardous waste management of its
環保專題制定KPI考核 ，每月制定固廢                            subsidiaries. During the pandemic in 2020, the Company

5. 和諧共贏                    5. All-win Harmony
 公司始終將員工作為企業發展的基石 ，堅信         The Company always regards employees as the cornerstone for
 「以效益體現價值 ，用財富回報才智」的人才        its development. Adhering to the talent concept of “reflecting
 理念 ，重視平等和非歧視的用工政策 ，切實        values with benefits and using wealth to reward intelligence”, the
 保障員工權益 ，完善員工晉升渠道及薪酬福         Company strongly advocates an equal and non-discriminatory
 利體系 ，持續對員工進行職業技能和職業健         employment policy, strives to protect the rights and interests of
 康培訓 ，不斷培養行業領軍人才 ，為員工打        employees and improves employee promotion channels as well
 造公平 、和諧 、包容的工作環境 ，攜手員        as remuneration and welfare system. Also, it constantly provides
 工共同進步 。                      employees with trainings on vocational skills and occupational
                              health and continuously nurtures industry leaders, creating a fair,
                              harmonious and inclusive working environment for employees to
                              grow together.


 5.1 尊重員工權益                   5.1 Respecting employees’ rights and interests
   東岳集團嚴格遵循以人為本的理念 ，               Dongyue Group adheres to the concept of “people-oriented”

   法律法規 ，堅持平等合規僱傭 ，公平              the People’s Republic of China and other national laws and
   對待不同性別 、地域 、年齡 、民族 、            regulations, pursued fair and legal employment practices,
   宗教和文化背景的員工 ，為所有員工               being impartial to employees different genders, nationalities,
   提供平等的工作機會 ，按國家規定為               ages, ethnicities, religions and cultural backgrounds. It
   所有聘用人員購買基本社會保險 。同               provides equal opportunities for all employees and purchases
   時 ，公司嚴禁和抵制任何形式的僱傭               basic social insurance for employees in accordance with
   童工 、強制勞工行為 ，並對僱傭童工              national regulations. At the same time, the Company strictly
   和強制勞工情況進行監督 ，重視和維               prohibits and opposes any use of child labor and forced
   護所有員工的合法權益 。截至報告期               labor, carries out supervision to ensure no employment of

附註：                                          Notes:

(a)    股份溢價                                  (a)      Share premium

       根據開曼群島公司法（經修訂），本公司的股份溢價賬可                      Under the Companies Law (Revised) of the Cayman Islands, the share premium
       分配給本公司所有者，惟在緊接建議分派股息之日後，                       of the Company is distributable to the owners of the Company provided that
       本公司將有能力償還其在日常業務過程中到期的債務。                       immediately following the date on which the dividend is proposed to be distributed,
                                                      the Company will be in a position to pay off its debts as they fall due in the ordinary
                                                      course of business.

(b)    按公平值計入其他全面收益的股本工具儲備                   (b)      Equity instruments at FVTOCI reserves

       按公平值計入其他全面收益的股本工具儲備包括於報告                       The equity instruments at FVTOCI reserves comprises the cumulative net change in
       期末根據香港財務報告準則第9號持有之按公平值計入其                      the fair value of equity instruments at FVTOCI under IFRS 9 that are held at the end of
       他全面收益之股本投資之公平值累計淨變動。                           the reporting period.

(c)    合併儲備                                  (c)      Merger reserve

       合併儲備來自於2006年完成之集團重組。                           Merger reserve arose in group reorganisation completed in 2006.

(d)    股本儲備                                  (d)      Capital reserve

       於2007年11月16日，本公司購回所有早前已發行每股面                   On 16 November 2007, the Company repurchased all of the 275,000,000 previously
       值0.1美元之普通股275,000,000股，而該等購回普通股與               issued ordinary shares of US$0.1 each and these repurchased ordinary shares were
       截至該日期之所有法定但未發行股本一併註銷。同日，                       cancelled with all of the authorised but unissued share capital as of that date. On
       藉增設4,000,000,000股每股面值0.1港元之新普通股，將法             the same date, the authorised share capital was increased to HK$400,000,000 by
       定股本增至400,000,000港元。於2007年11月15日，向現有            the creation of 4,000,000,000 new ordinary shares of HK$0.1 each. 275,000,000 new
       股東發行275,000,000股每股面值0.1港元之新普通股。購               ordinary shares of HK$0.1 each were then issued to the shareholders existing on 15
       回金額超過已發行新股份面值之款額直接計入股本儲備。                      November 2007. The excess of the repurchased amount over the nominal amount of
                                                      new shares issued was credited directly to the capital reserve.

       向附屬公司非控股股東收購額外權益確認為與非控股股                       The acquisitions of additional interest from non-controlling shareholders of
       東權益之交易，因此產生之相關折讓╱溢價直接於股本                       subsidiaries were recognised as transactions with non-controlling interests and the
       儲備列賬╱支銷。                                       corresponding discount/premium were credited/debited directly against capital
                                                      reserve.

(e)    安全儲備                                  (e)      Safety reserve

       根據中華人民共和國（「中國」）財政部及國家安全生產監                     Pursuant to certain regulations issued by the Ministry of Finance and the State
       督管理總局頒佈的若干法規，本集團之若干中國附屬公                       Administration of Work Safety of the People’s Republic of China (“PRC”), some the
       司須以累進比率按危險化學品銷售總收入之0.5%至4%預                    Group’s PRC subsidiaries are required to set aside an amount of safety reserve at
       留一定數額之安全儲備金。該儲備可用作集團日常運作                       progressive rates from 0.5% to 4% of the total revenue from the sales of hazardous
       於安全生產改善及維修之用，被視為開支性質，並於產                       chemical. The reserve can be utilised for the spending in improvements and
       生時計入損益。                                        maintenances of work safety on the Group’s daily operations, which are considered
                                                      expenses in nature and charged to the profit and loss as incurred.

(f)    法定盈餘儲備                                (f)      Statutory surplus reserve

       根據中華人民共和國（「中國」）公司法及相關公司組織章                     In accordance with the Company Law of People’s Republic of China (“PRC”) and the
       程細則，本公司中國附屬公司須將相等於根據中國會計                       relevant Articles of Association, the PRC subsidiaries of the Company are required
       準則釐定之除稅後溢利10%之金額撥入法定盈餘儲備。                      to appropriate amount equal to 10% of their profit after taxation as determined in
                                                      accordance with the PRC accounting standards to the statutory surplus reserve.

       法定盈餘儲備為股東權益之一部分，當其結餘達到相當                       Statutory surplus reserve is part of shareholders’ equity and when its balance reaches
       於註冊資本50%之金額，則毋須進一步分配。根據中國公                     an amount equal to 50% of the registered capital, further appropriation is not
       司法，法定盈餘儲備可用作填補過往虧損、增加生產及                       required. According to the Company Law of the PRC, statutory surplus reserve may
       業務營運或轉增股本。                                     be used to make up past losses, to increase production and business operations or to
                                                      increase capital by means of conversion.

(g)    截至2020年12月31日止年度，本集團進一步向少數股東          (g)      During the year ended 31 December 2020, the Group further acquired 36% of the
       收購山東東岳聯邦置業有限公司（「聯邦置業」）36%股權。                   equity interests of Shandong Dongyue Union Property Co., Ltd. (“Union Property”),
       於2020年12月30日，聯邦置業成為本集團之全資附屬公                   from the minority shareholders. As at 30 December 2020, Union Property became a
       司。                                             wholly-owned subsidiary of the Group.

 東岳集團有限公司（「本公司」）於2006年7月                         Dongyue Group Limited (the “Company”) was incorporated in
 24日於開曼群島註冊成立為受豁免有限公                             the Cayman Islands on 24 July 2006 as an exempted company
 司，其註冊辦事處位於P.O. Box 31119, Grand                 with limited liability. The address of its registered office is P.O. Box
 Pavilion, Hibiscus Way, 802 West Bay Road,      31119, Grand Pavilion, Hibiscus Way, 802 West Bay Road, Grand
 Grand Cayman, KY1-1205, Cayman Islands。本        Cayman, KY1-1205, Cayman Islands. The shares of the Company
 公司的股份自2007年12月10日起在香港聯合                         have been listed on the Main Board of The Stock Exchange of
 交易所有限公司主板上市。                                    Hong Kong Limited since 10 December 2007.


 本公司為一間投資控股公司。本公司及其附                             The Company is an investment holding company. The Company
 屬公司（合稱「本集團」）主要從事製造、分銷                           and its subsidiaries (collectively referred to as the “Group”) are
 及銷售高分子材料、有機硅、製冷劑及二氯                             principally engaged in the manufacture, distribution and sale
 甲烷、聚氯乙烯（「PVC」）及燒鹼以及其他。                          of polymers, organic silicone, refrigerants and dichloromethane,
 此外，本集團亦在中華人民共和國（「中國」）                           polyvinyl chloride (“PVC”) and liquid alkali and others. In addition,
 從事物業開發。                                         the Group is also engaged in property development in the
                                                 People’s Republic of China (the “PRC”).


 綜合財務報表以本公司及其附屬公司之功能                             The consolidated financial statements are presented in Renminbi

         由於本集團不採用簡易處理方                            The application is not expected to have impact on

3. 主要會計政策                                3. Significant accounting policies
       合規聲明                                  Statement of compliance
       綜合財務報表乃根據國際會計準則理事會頒                   The consolidated financial statements have been prepared in
       佈的國際財務報告準則編製。另外，綜合財                   accordance with International Financial Reporting Standards
       務報表包括香港聯合交易所有限公司證券上                   issued by IASB. In addition, the consolidated financial statements
       市規則及香港《公司條例》規定之適用披露。                  include applicable disclosures required by The Rules Governing
                                             the Listing of Securities on The Stock Exchange of Hong Kong
                                             Limited and by the Hong Kong Companies Ordinance.


       報告編製依據                                Basis of preparation
       綜合財務報表乃根據國際會計準則理事會頒                   The consolidated financial statements have been prepared in
       佈的國際財務報告準則編製。另外，綜合財                   accordance with International Financial Reporting Standards issued
       務報表包括香港聯合交易所有限公司證券上                   by International Accounting Standards Board. In addition, the
       市規則及香港公司條例規定之適用披露。                    consolidated financial statements include applicable disclosures
                                             required by the Rules Governing the Listing of Securities on The
                                             Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited and by the Hong Kong
                                             Companies Ordinance.


       綜合財務報表乃按歷史成本基準編製，惟金                   The consolidated financial statements have been prepared on
       融工具於各報告期末乃按公平值計入其他全                   the historical cost basis except for financial instruments that are
       面收益計量。歷史成本一般按交換貨品及服                   measured at fair value through other comprehensive income at
       務之代價之公平值計算。                           the end of each reporting period. Historical cost is generally based
                                             on the fair value of the consideration given in exchange for goods
                                             and services.


       公平值指於計量日期市場參與者於有秩序交                   Fair value is the price that would be received to sell an asset or
       易中出售一項資產可收取或轉讓一項負債須                   paid to transfer a liability in an orderly transaction between market
       支付之價格，不論該價格是否直接可觀察或                   participants at the measurement date, regardless of whether that
       可使用其他估值方法作出估計。倘市場參與                   price is directly observable or estimated using another valuation
       者於計量日期對資產或負債定價時會考慮資                   technique. In estimating the fair value of an asset or a liability,

 此外，出於財務報告目的，公平值計量應基        In addition, for financial reporting purposes, fair value
 於公平值計量的輸入值的可觀察程度以及該        measurements are categorised into Level 1, 2 or 3 based on the
 等輸入值對整體公平值計量的重要性，被歸        degree to which the inputs to the fair value measurements are
 入第一層、第二層或第三層的公平值級次，        observable and the significance of the inputs to the fair value
 詳述如下：                      measurement in its entirety, which are described as follows:


 第一層輸入值是指於計量日期由相同資產或        Level 1 inputs are quoted prices (unadjusted) in active markets
 負債在活躍市場中（未經調整）的報價得出；       for identical assets or liabilities that the entity can access at the
 第二層輸入值是指由除了第一層輸入值所包        measurement date; Level 2 inputs are inputs, other than quoted
 含的報價以外的，資產或負債可由直接或間        prices included within Level 1, that are observable for the asset
 接觀察得出的輸入值；及第三層輸入值是指        or liability, either directly or indirectly; and Level 3 inputs are
 資產或負債的不可觀察輸入值。             unobservable inputs for the asset or liability.

 綜合基準                       Basis of consolidation
 綜合財務報表包括本公司及由本公司及其附        The consolidated financial statements incorporate the financial
 屬公司控制的實體的財務報表。當本公司具        statements of the Company and entities controlled by the
 備以下條件時，即具有控制權：             Company and its subsidiaries. Control is achieved when the
                            Company:


 －   有控制被投資方的權力；            –    has power over the investee;


 －   可或有權自其與被投資方的關係中獲得      –    is exposed, or has rights, to variable returns from its
     不同回報；及                      involvement with the investee; and


 －   有能力運用其權力影響其回報。         –    has the ability to use its power to affect its returns.


 倘事實和情況顯示上述的三項控制因素之一        The Group reassesses whether or not it controls an investee if
 項或多項出現變動，本集團會重新評估其是        facts and circumstances indicate that there are changes to one or
 否控制被投資方。                   more of the three elements of control listed above.

 客戶合約收益                     Revenue from contracts with customers
 本集團於完成履約責任時，即於特定履約責        The Group recognises revenue when (or as) a performance
 任相關的貨品或服務的「控制權」轉讓予客戶       obligation is satisfied, i.e. when “control” of the goods or services
 時確認收益。                     underlying the particular performance obligation is transferred to
                            the customer.


 履約責任指可明確區分的一項貨品或服務         A performance obligation represents a good or service (or a
 （或一組貨品或服務），或一系列大致相同的       bundle of goods or services) that is distinct or a series of distinct
 可明確區分的貨品或服務。               goods or services that are substantially the same.


 倘符合以下其中一項標準，則控制權隨時間        Control is transferred over time and revenue is recognised over
 轉移，而收益則參照完全履行相關履約責任        time by reference to the progress towards complete satisfaction of
 的進展情況而隨時間確認：               the relevant performance obligation if one of the following criteria
                            is met:


 －   客戶在本集團履約的同時即取得並消耗      –    the customer simultaneously receives and consumes the
     本集團履約所帶來之利益；                benefits provided by the Group’s performance as the Group
                                 performs;


 －   本集團履約導致產生及提升一項資產，      –    the Group’s performance creates or enhances an asset that
     而該項資產於產生或提升時由客戶控            the customer controls as the Group performs; or
     制；或


 －   本集團履約未產生對本集團具有替代用      –    the Group’s performance does not create an asset with
     途之資產，而本集團有執行權收取迄今           an alternative use to the Group and the Group has an
     已履約部分之款項。                   enforceable right to payment for performance completed to
                                 date.


 否則，收益於客戶獲得特定貨品或服務的控        Otherwise, revenue is recognised at a point in time when the
 制權時確認。                     customer obtains control of the distinct good or service.

       租賃的定義                                 Definition of a lease
       倘合約授予權利以代價為交換在某一時期內                   A contract is, or contains, a lease if the contract conveys the right
       控制使用已識別資產，則該合約屬於租賃或                   to control the use of an identified asset for a period of time in
       包含租賃。                                 exchange for consideration.


       就於初步應用日期或之後訂立或修訂或由業                   For contracts entered into or modified or arising from business
       務合併產生的合約而言，本集團根據國際財                   combinations on or after the date of initial application, the
       務報告準則第16號中的定義，於開始、修訂                  Group assesses whether a contract is or contains a lease based
       日期或收購日期評估合約是否為一項租賃或                   on the definition under IFRS 16 at inception, modification date
       包含一項租賃。除非合約條款及條件其後出                   or acquisition date, as appropriate. Such contract will not be
       現變動，否則有關合約將不予重新評估。                    reassessed unless the terms and conditions of the contract are
                                             subsequently changed.


       本集團作為承租人                              The Group as a lessee
       就可行實際權宜方法而言，當本集團合理預                   As a practical expedient, leases with similar characteristics are
       期對綜合財務報表的影響將不會與投資組合                   accounted on a portfolio basis when the Group reasonably
       內個別租賃有重大差異時，具有相似特徵的                   expects that the effects on the consolidated financial statements
       租賃將以組合為基礎入賬。                          would not differ materially from individual leases within the

 使用權資產的成本包括：                The cost of right-of-use asset includes:

     額；

     所在地點或將相關資產恢復至租賃條款           dismantling and removing the underlying assets, restoring
     及條件所要求的狀況時所產生的估計成           the site on which it is located or restoring the underlying
     本。                          asset to the condition required by the terms and conditions
                                 of the lease.


 使用權資產按成本減去任何累計折舊及減值        Right-of-use assets are measured at cost, less any accumulated
 損失計量，並對租賃負債的任何重新計量作        depreciation and impairment losses, and adjusted for any
 出調整。                       remeasurement of lease liabilities.


 本集團合理確定於租期結束時取得相關租賃        Right-of-use assets in which the Group is reasonably certain to
 資產擁有權的使用權資產，由開始日期起至        obtain ownership of the underlying leased assets at the end of the

 按其估計可使用年期及租期兩者中較短者以        of the useful life. Otherwise, right-of-use assets are depreciated on
 直線法折舊。                     a straight-line basis over the shorter of its estimated useful life and
                            the lease term.

3. 主要會計政策（續）           3. Significant accounting policies (Continued)
 於聯營公司的投資                 Investment in associates
 聯營公司為本集團對其有重大影響力的實       An associate is an entity over which the Group has significant
 體。重大影響力指有權參與被投資方的財務      influence. Significant influence is the power to participate in the
 及經營政策的決策，但對該等政策並無控制      financial and operating policy decisions of the investee but is not
 或共同控制權。                  control or joint control over those policies.


 聯營公司之業績、資產及負債使用權益會計      The results and assets and liabilities of associates are incorporated
 法計入此等綜合財務報表。以權益會計法處      in these consolidated financial statements using the equity
 理的聯營公司財務報表乃按本集團就於類似      method of accounting. The financial statements of associates
 情況下的交易及事件所採用者相同的會計政      used for equity accounting purposes are prepared using uniform
 策編製。根據權益法，於聯營公司之投資初      accounting policies as those of the Group for like transactions
 步按成本於綜合財務狀況表確認，並於其後      and events in similar circumstances. Under the equity method,
 就確認本集團應佔該聯營公司之損益及其他      an investment in an associate is initially recognised in the

 淨額一部分之任何長期權益），本集團終止      Group’s share of losses of an associate exceeds the Group’s

 於被投資方成為一間聯營公司當日，對聯營      An investment in an associate is accounted for using the equity

 金融工具                      Financial instruments
 當本集團實體成為工具合約條文的訂約方        Financial assets and financial liabilities are recognised when a
 時，確認金融資產及金融負債。所有以常規       group entity becomes a party to the contractual provisions of the
 方式購入或出售之金融資產均按買賣日期基       instrument. All regular way purchases or sales of financial assets
 準確認及終止確認。以常規方式購入或出售       are recognised and derecognised on a trade date basis. Regular
 指須於市場規定或慣例所訂時限內交付資產       way purchases or sales are purchases or sales of financial assets
 之金融資產購入或出售。               that require delivery of assets within the time frame established by
                           regulation or convention in the market place.


 金融資產及金融負債初步按公平值計量，惟       Financial assets and financial liabilities are initially measured at
 按國際財務報告準則第15號初步計量之貿易      fair value except for trade receivables which are initially measured
 應收款項除外。收購或發行金融資產及金融       in accordance with IFRS 15. Transaction costs that are directly
 負債（按公平值計入損益（「按公平值計入損      attributable to the acquisition or issue of financial assets and
 益」）之金融資產或金融負債除外）的直接應      financial liabilities (other than financial assets or financial liabilities
 佔交易成本加入或自初步確認之金融資產或       at fair value through profit or loss (“FVTPL”) are added to or
 金融負債（倘合適）的公平值扣除。收購按公      deducted from the fair value of the financial assets or financial
 平值計入損益之金融資產或金融負債直接應       liabilities, as appropriate, on initial recognition. Transaction costs
 佔之交易成本乃即時於損益確認。           directly attributable to the acquisition of financial assets or
                           financial liabilities at FVTPL are recognised immediately in profit or
                           loss.


 實際利息法是一種計算金融資產或金融負債       The effective interest method is a method of calculating the
 的攤銷成本及於有關期間攤分利息收入及利       amortised cost of a financial asset or financial liability and of
 息開支的方法。實際利率是於初步確認時準       allocating interest income and interest expense over the relevant
 確地透過金融資產或金融負債預計年期或        period. The effective interest rate is the rate that exactly discounts
 （如適用）於較短期間內將估計日後現金收款      estimated future cash receipts and payments (including all fees
 及付款（包括屬整體實際利率一部分的所有       and points paid or received that form an integral part of the
 已付或已收費用及利息、交易成本及其他溢       effective interest rate, transaction costs and other premiums or
 價或折讓）貼現為賬面淨值的利率。          discounts) through the expected life of the financial asset or
                           financial liability, or, where appropriate, a shorter period, to the
                           net carrying amount on initial recognition.

 (iii) 信貸減值金融資產               (iii) Credit-impaired financial assets
       金融資產在一項或以上事件（對該金融            A financial asset is credit-impaired when one or more events
       資產估計未來現金流量構成不利影響）            that have a detrimental impact on the estimated future cash
       發生時維持信貸減值。金融資產維持信            flows of that financial asset have occurred. Evidence that a
       貸減值的證據包括有關下列事件的可觀            financial asset is credit-impaired includes observable data
       察數據：                         about the following events:


   －   發行人或借款人的重大財務困難；             –    significant financial difficulty of the issuer or the
                                        borrower;


   －   違反合約，如違約或逾期事件；              –    a breach of contract, such as a default or past due
                                        event;


   －   借款人的貸款人因有關借款人財              –    the lender(s) of the borrower, for economic or
       務困難的經濟或合約理由而向借                   contractual reasons relating to the borrower’s
       款人授予貸款人不會另行考慮的                   financial difficulty, having granted to the borrower a
       優惠；                              concession(s) that the lender(s) would not otherwise
                                        consider;


   －   借款人將可能陷入破產或其他財              –    it is becoming probable that the borrower will enter
       務重組；或                            bankruptcy or other financial reorganisation; or


   －   該金融資產的活躍市場因財務困              –    the disappearance of an active market for that financial
       難而消失。                            asset because of financial difficulties.

       (iv) 撇銷政策                             (iv) Write-off policy
            資料顯示對手方處於嚴重財務困難及無                     The Group writes off a financial asset when there is
            實際收回可能時（例如，當對手方處於                     information indicating that the counterparty is in severe
            清盤狀態或進入破產程序時）（以較早發                    financial difficulty and there is no realistic prospect of
            生者為準），本集團則撇銷金融資產。                     recovery, for example, when the counterparty has been
                                                  placed under liquidation or has entered into bankruptcy
                                                  proceedings, whichever occurs sooner.


              經考慮法律意見後（倘合適），遭撇銷的                   Financial assets written off may still be subject to
              金融資產可能仍須按本集團收回程序進                    enforcement activities under the Group’s recovery
              行強制執行活動。撇銷構成取消確認事                    procedures, taking into account legal advice where
              項。任何其後收回在損益中確認。                      appropriate. A write-off constitutes a derecognition event.
                                                   Any subsequent recoveries are recognised in profit or loss.


       (v)    預期信貸虧損的計量及確認                   (v)   Measurement and recognition of ECL
              預期信貸虧損的計量為違約概率、違約                    The measurement of ECL is a function of the probability
              損失率（即違約損失程度）及違約風險的                   of default, loss given default (i.e. the magnitude of the
              函數。違約概率及違約損失率乃基於根                    loss if there is a default) and the exposure at default. The
              據前瞻性資料調整的歷史數據評估。預                    assessment of the probability of default and loss given
              期信貸虧損的預估乃無偏概率加權平均                    default is based on historical data adjusted by forward-
              金額，以發生違約的風險為權重確定。                    looking information. Estimation of ECL reflects an unbiased
                                                   and probability-weighted amount that is determined with
                                                   the respective risks of default occurring as the weights.


              一般而言，預期信貸虧損按本集團根據                    Generally, the ECL is the difference between all contractual
              合約應收的所有合約現金流量與本集團                    cash flows that are due to the Group in accordance with
              預計收取的現金流量的差額估計，並按                    the contract and the cash flows that the Group expects to
              初始確認時釐定的實際利率貼現。                      receive, discounted at the effective interest rate determined
                                                   at initial recognition.

 於應用附註3所述的本集團會計政策時，本      In the application of the Group’s accounting policies, which are
 公司董事須就並不容易從其他來源顯示的資      described in note 3, the directors of the Company are required
 產及負債的賬面值作出估計及假設。估計及      to make estimates and assumptions about the carrying amounts
 相關假設乃根據過往經驗及其他被認為相關      of assets and liabilities that are not readily apparent from other
 的因素作出。實際結果可能與該等估計不       sources. The estimates and associated assumptions are based on
 同。                       historical experience and other factors that are considered to be
                          relevant. Actual results may differ from these estimates.


 估計及相關假設會持續檢討。倘會計估計的      The estimates and underlying assumptions are reviewed on an
 修訂僅對估計修訂的期間構成影響，則有關      ongoing basis. Revisions to accounting estimates are recognised
 修訂於該期間確認，或倘有關修訂對本期間      in the period in which the estimate is revised if the revision affects
 及未來期間均構成影響，則修訂會於該修訂      only that period, or in the period of the revision and future periods
 期間及未來期間確認。               if the revision affects both current and future periods.

       易及其他應收款項使用相關信貸虧損）釐定                        impairment of trade receivables based on the expected credit

              信貸風險                               Credit risk
              本集團並無重大集中的信貸風險。銀行                  The Group has no significant concentrations of credit risk.
              結餘及現金、已抵押銀行存款及貿易應                  The carrying amounts of bank balances and cash, pledged
              收款項的賬面金額反映本集團金融資產                  bank deposits and trade receivables represent the Group’s
              的最高信貸風險。於2020年及2019年12             maximum exposure to credit risk in relation to financial
              月31日，所有銀行結餘均存入國有銀行                 assets. As at 31 December 2020 and 2019, all bank balances
              及信譽良好的金融機構，故無重大信貸                  were deposited in reputable financial institutions and were
              風險。管理層預期不會因該等對手方不                  hence without significant credit risk. Management does
              履約而招致任何損失。信貸銷售僅提供                  not expect any losses from non-performance by these
              予信貸記錄良好的選定客戶。本集團已                  counterparties. Credit sales are made to selected customers
              制訂政策確保及時追收未償還的貿易應                  with good credit history. The Group has policies in place to
              收款項。貿易應收款項須應用預期信貸                  ensure that outstanding trade receivables are collected on
              虧損模式。本集團應用國際財務報告準                  a timely basis. Trade receivables are subject to the expected
              則第9號簡化方法計量預期信貸虧損，                  credit loss model. The Group applies IFRS 9 simplified
              於初步確認時，為所有貿易應收款項撥                  approach to measuring expected credit losses which uses a
              備整個存續期內的預期虧損。為計量預                  lifetime expected loss allowance for all trade receivables from
              期信貸虧損，貿易應收款項已根據應佔                  initial recognition. To measure the expected credit losses,
              信貸風險特點及過期天數分類。預期虧                  trade receivables have been grouped based on shared credit
              損率基於銷售的歷史付款情況及出現的                  risk characteristics and the days past due. The expected loss
              相應過往信貸虧損。過往虧損率作出調                  rates are based on the historical payment profiles of sales
              整以反映影響客戶結算應收賬款能力的                  and the corresponding historical credit losses experienced.
              因素的當前及前瞻性資料。                       The historical loss rates are adjusted to reflect current and
                                                 forward-looking information on factors affecting the ability of
                                                 the customers to settle the receivables.


              其他應收款項（不包括預付款項）的信貸                 The credit quality of the other receivables excluding
              質素已參考對手方的違約率的歷史資料                  prepayments has been assessed with reference to historical

              為，其他應收款項的信貸風險較低，原                  financial position of the counterparties. The directors are of
              因是應收款項的收回記錄良好。董事相                  the opinion that the credit risk of other receivables is low

       6.2 經營分部                              6.2 Operating Segments

              本集團業務乃按產品及物業開發的類型                      The Group’s operations are organised based on the different
              劃分。向本公司董事會（即總營運決策                      types of products and property development. Information
              人）
               （「總營運決策人」）報告以作資源分                     reported to the board of directors of the Company, being the
              配及分部表現評估的資料亦按產品及物                      chief operating decision maker (“CODM”), for the purposes of
              業開發的類型呈列。此乃本集團之構造                      resource allocation and assessment of segment performance
              基礎。                                    focuses on types of products and property development.
                                                     This is the basis upon which the Group is organised.


              本集團之經營及呈報分部如下：                         The Group’s operating and reportable segments are as
                                                     follows:


              －      高分子材料；                      –        Polymers;

                     省發展住宅物業。                             properties in Shandong and Hunan Province, the PRC.


              －      其他業務－生產及銷售製冷劑分              –        Other operations – manufacturing and sales of side-
                     部、高分子材料分部、有機硅分                       products of refrigerants segment, polymers segment,
                     部及二氯甲烷、PVC及燒鹼分部的                     organic silicone segment and dichloromethane, PVC

 股本工具（續）                                           other comprehensive income (Continued)
 於釐定非上市股本工具公平值時，本集團委                               In determining the fair value of unlisted equity instruments, the
 聘獨立專業估值師進行有關估值。該金額乃                               Group engages an independent professional valuer to perform
 根據估計未來現金流量貼現至其現值的現金                               such valuation. The amount is determined based on the cash flow
 流量預測釐定，並需要使用主要假設，包括                               projection for the estimated future cash flow discounted to its
 貼現率、最終增長率、預算收益及毛利率，                               present value and requires the use of key assumptions, including
 並考慮到相關行業增長預測及管理層就對市                               the discount rate, terminal growth rate, budgeted revenue
 場發展的期望所批准的財務預算。                                   and gross margin taking into account the relevant industry
                                                   growth forecasts and financial budgets approved management’s
                                                   expectation for the market development.

36. 退休福利計劃                                  36. Retirement benefits plans
       本集團在中國的僱員乃中國各個地方政府營                     The employees of the Group in the PRC are members of state-
       運的國家退休福利計劃的成員。本集團須向                     managed retirement benefit schemes operated by the respective
       計劃供款指定百分比（按薪酬成本計算），作                    local governments in the PRC. The Group is required to contribute
       為有關福利的資金。本集團的唯一責任是對                     a specified percentage of payroll costs to the schemes to fund the
       該等計劃作出指定供款。                             benefits. The only obligation of the Group with respect to these
                                               schemes is to make the specified contributions.

39. 視為部分出售一間附屬公司                              39. Deemed partial disposal of a subsidiary
       於2020年3月12日，本集團完成山東東岳有                      On 12 March 2020, the Group has completed the spin-off
       機硅材料股份有限公司（「東岳有機硅」）的                        of Shangdong Dongyue Organosilicone Co., Ltd (“Dongyue
       股票在深圳證券交易所的創業板分拆上市                          Organosilicone”), an indirect non-wholly owned subsidiary of
      （「分拆」），該公司是本公司的間接非全資                         the Company engaged in manufacture and sale of organosilicon
       附屬公司，從事有機硅材料的生產和銷售。                         material, through a separate listing of its shares on the ChiNext
       分 拆 涉 及 以 發 行 價 每 股 人 民 幣6.90元 發 行          of Shenzhen Stock Exchange (“Spin-Off”). The Spin-Off involved
       300,000,000股每股面值人民幣1元的新A股，                  offering of 300,000,000 new A-share of RMB1 each at an issue
       總計籌得約人民幣1,985,849,000元的淨現金                  price of RMB6.90 per share, which raised a total net cash proceeds
       收益。                                         of approximately RMB1,985,849,000.


       緊接分拆完成後，本集團在東岳有機硅的股                         Immediately following the completion of the Spin-Off, the Group’s
       權隨即從77%攤薄至57.75%，因此分拆被視                     equity interest in Dongyue Organosilicone was diluted from
       為部分出售。由於視為部分出售東岳有機硅                         77% to 57.75% and thus the Spin-Off is considered as a deemed
       不會導致失去控制權，因此該交易作股權交                         partial disposal. Since the deemed partial disposal of Dongyue
       易入賬，而分拆所得淨額與當時東岳有機                          Organosilicone did not result in any loss of control, the transaction
       硅42.25%賬面值的差額被確認為本集團的權                      was accounted for as an equity transaction and the difference
       益。                                          between the net proceeds from the Spin-Off and the then 42.25%
                                                   carrying value of Dongyue Organosilicone is recognised in the
                                                   equity of the Group.


       分拆對財務影響總結如下：                                A summary of the financial impacts of the Spin-Off are as follows:


                                                                                                           人民幣千元
